# Layout

[Back to the README](../README.md)

The library [`AmbidextrousSofa/`](../AmbidextrousSofa) has 466 modules: the plane, motions, caps and areas in
`Geometry/`, `Measure/`, `Topology/` and `Algebra/`; Romik's ambidextrous sofa in `Reference/`; the bound on
the score of a cap and its equality cases, the bulk of the manuscript, in `Analysis/`; the main theorems in
[`Theorems/Optimality.lean`](../AmbidextrousSofa/Theorems/Optimality.lean), and in the vocabulary of the Challenge
in [`Main.lean`](../AmbidextrousSofa/Main.lean), with the definitions that the Challenge copies in
[`ChallengeDefs.lean`](../AmbidextrousSofa/ChallengeDefs.lean). [`Analysis/SurfaceAreaFacts.lean`](../AmbidextrousSofa/Analysis/SurfaceAreaFacts.lean)
proves three facts about the surface area measure of a convex body that lean4-moving-sofa proves privately.

This page maps the modules to the sections of the [manuscript](archive/README.md); the links `L…` point to its
lines. Section 1 lists the modules of each section, with the main Lean statements of each technical proof,
Section 2 the sections that no module formalizes or that the formalization reaches by another route, and
Section 3 every module. The Lean proofs are those of ChatGPT Pro 6's Lean draft of the manuscript's argument;
"formalizes" means that a module proves the section's result or a step of it, sometimes by a different route
(Section 2). The map was made by reading the modules and the manuscript, not by a mechanical check.

Contents: [1. The modules of each section](#1-the-modules-of-each-section-of-the-manuscript) ·
[2. Sections not formalized, or formalized differently](#2-sections-not-formalized-or-formalized-differently) ·
[3. Every module](#3-every-module)

Conventions: `Ch k §n` is a section of Chapters 1 to 5, and `TPn CODE §x` a subsection of Technical proof n,
with its code (AF, SR, …). TP1 to TP3 keep the numbers of the notes they come from (Note 14, Note 4,
Note 13).

## 1. The modules of each section of the manuscript

Each line lists the modules whose section column (Section 3) names that section. Chapters 3-5 are integrated summaries of the technical proofs: only the assembly modules are listed under them, and the step-by-step formalization appears under the corresponding technical proof. "—" means no module is assigned; Section 2 comments on these. Under each technical proof, "Main Lean statements" lists the declarations that state its main results, where they can be identified, together with the module that declares them.

## 2. Sections not formalized, or formalized differently

### 2.1 Mathematical sections with no Lean counterpart

| Section | Lines | Remark |
|---|---|---|
| Ch2 §4 The independent width-five box calculation | [L571–597](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L571-L597) | GA.9-GA.11 (W < 5, hull inside [-5/2,5/2]×[0,1]) are not stated. The Lean reduction never boxes the original body: compactness is obtained at the cap level from SD.10 (`IsDownwardCap.competitive_width_bounds`) and centering. |
| Ch2 §5 Connectedification of arbitrary auxiliary envelopes | [L599–634](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L599-L634) | GA.12-GA.15 are not formalized. The Lean argument applies the signed-fiber bound only to the actual connected body ([`Measure/Partition`](../AmbidextrousSofa/Measure/Partition.lean), [`Measure/CapAssembly`](../AmbidextrousSofa/Measure/CapAssembly.lean)), so no auxiliary envelope has to be made connected. |
| TP3 §13.1 The functional suggested by the three-piece niche boundary | [L2524–2559](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L2524-L2559) | The switching functionals F_{a,b} and Q_{a,b} (13.2-13.3) are not defined; the formalization uses the adaptive functional of TP4 instead. TP3 §13.2 is covered only through its identities 13.4-13.5, which appear as determinant identities in [`Measure/SignedRoofIntegral`](../AmbidextrousSofa/Measure/SignedRoofIntegral.lean). |
| TP4 §A.4 Hamiltonian form and the period bound | [L2768–2792](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L2768-L2792) | No Hamiltonian H(P,Q) or period estimate. The stationary classification (A.5-A.7) is re-proved with an explicit stationary family covering every competitive width (`AdaptiveGlobalFamily`, `AdaptiveGlobalParameters`, `AdaptiveGlobalScalar`, `AdaptiveGlobalBranches`), combined with fixed-trace uniqueness. |
| TP18 Part I A first exact width exclusion | [L5966–6022](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L5966-L6022) | The horizontal-specific proof of C < 13/15 is not formalized. Its conclusion is covered by TW Part C (`IsFullCapMaximizer.width_lt_sixteen_fifths`), which applies to horizontal maximizers too. |
| TP18 Part III Wide-width exclusion by three actual niche angles (statement and §1-§5) | [L6336–6593](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L6336-L6593) | Not formalized as a separate horizontal argument. The excluded range C >= 4/5 is covered by the same TW Part C theorem. |
| TP19 Part A §1 Raise the left wing to a genuine horizontal-middle cap | [L6633–6687](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L6633-L6687) | The auxiliary lifted cap is deliberately not constructed. The same device appears in TP19 Part D §1 and TP20 §1, whose remaining content is formalized. `DiagonalRelaxation`, `TiltedDiagonalRoof` and `FullTriangleParameters` derive the needed bounds from the original cap's supports. |
| TP21 §5 A stronger left-boundary signed margin when C <= 1/sqrt(2) | [L8366–8398](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L8366-L8398) | FG11 (F(-C) <= -epsilon) is not stated. The dry left endpoint comes from the reflected-arc argument instead (`FirstUnitTopOverhang`, `TiltedTopOverhang`). |
| TP33 §8 The stationary clipped Green identity | [L12741–12813](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L12741-L12813) | PS.5 (2P_alpha = L_wing and max_J q <= (e_R+e_L)/2 at a proper angle) has no counterpart. The Green identity and niche-height bound are formalized only for full caps (`SelectedGreenLimit`, `SelectedNicheHeight`). |
| TP33 §9.1 Terminal-only historical ties have full occupation | [L12862–12911](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L12862-L12911) | PS.36 is not formalized, and neither is the weak-star occupation chi of PS.34-PS.35. `TerminalExposure` instead bounds strict terminal exposure by the facet width through an actual inward cut. |
| TP39 §1 Statement and normalization; §4 A first-unit wing forces the companion wing to be unit | [L14510–14577](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L14510-L14577), [L14841–14936](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L14841-L14936) | PU's conditional exclusion is not formalized. The proper-angle case is closed through RP/RX/AC/MP (`ProperTerminalExclusion`). PU §2 (local laws) and §3 (short terminal facet) are formalized. |
| TP46 §4 An independent global strip obstruction for the reference hull | [L17466–17518](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L17466-L17518) | Lemma TB3 / G3C.12 (width > 1 in every non-vertical direction) is not stated. The full-angle conclusion comes from TB2 (`IsDownwardCap.capScore_eq_half_M_iff`) instead. |

### 2.2 Sections without formalizable mathematical content

These are expository, scope or record sections; no module is expected to formalize them.

- Reading guide ([L4–68](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L4-L68)) and the Abstract of Chapter 1 ([L75–106](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L75-L106)).
- Ch1 §5 Notation and dependency conventions ([L378–403](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L378-L403)). The pressure notation Q, C_R, C_L appears as definitions in [`Analysis/SelectedPressureLimit`](../AmbidextrousSofa/Analysis/SelectedPressureLimit.lean) and [`Analysis/PartialPressureLimit`](../AmbidextrousSofa/Analysis/PartialPressureLimit.lean).
- TP44 §7 Scope and verification ([L16817–16833](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L16817-L16833)).
- TP46 §1 The equality-selection issue ([L17175–17195](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L17175-L17195)) and §6 What happens to measure-zero changes ([L17614–17646](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L17614-L17646)). The issues they raise are handled by `TerminalFacetAtom` and [`Topology/RegularClosed`](../AmbidextrousSofa/Topology/RegularClosed.lean).
- TP47 §3 What strict functional calibration does and does not require ([L17809–17857](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L17809-L17857)), §5 A largest-angle choice is not an equality theorem ([L17923–17963](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L17923-L17963)) and §7 What a manuscript must expose ([L18040–18071](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L18040-L18071)). The concern of §5 is addressed by `TerminalFacetAtom` and `ProperAngleStrictness`.
- External-input cross-reference ([L18076–18078](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L18076-L18078)) and Verification and review record ([L18081–18087](https://github.com/vltanh/lean4-ambidextrous-sofa/blob/main/docs/archive/GATE3-MANUSCRIPT.md?plain=1#L18081-L18087)).

### 2.3 Sections formalized only in part or in a different form

- TP2 §4.3, Theorem 13: only the strict ceiling (apex height < 1/2, `Reference.referenceApexHeight_lt_half`) is formalized. The exact maximum q(pi/4) = 1/2 + R - sqrt2 and its uniqueness are not stated.
- TP4 AF1: the existence of an H^1 maximizer at fixed width (weak compactness) is not formalized. Only strict concavity and fixed-trace uniqueness for C^1 `QuarterPair`s are proved (`AdaptiveFunctional`, `QuadraticEnergy`, `DirichletEnergy`). The A.6 attainment argument is likewise replaced by the explicit family.
- TP16 §8 (Theorem CH7): its conclusion (first-quarter unit curvature for tilted C <= 2/3) is reached through the IE criterion in `TiltedUnitSelection`, not through the CH §8 post-jump energy argument.
- TP31 Theorem G1C2 (full-turn body bound): there is no separate statement; it is subsumed by `IsAmbidextrous.volumeReal_le_M`.
- TP40 RP: its final exclusion of 0 < cos a <= 1/25 is not stated separately. RX (cot a <= 1/8) and AC cover all proper angles in `ProperTerminalExclusion`.
- TP22 (FO) §7 (independent algebra check): the FO9 identities are proved inside `FirstUnitFoldBudget` and `TiltedFirstUnitEnergy` (listed in Part 2), not as a separate check.

## 3. Every module

Sorted by directory, then by module name. Counts: root 3, Algebra 1, Analysis 409, Geometry 15, Measure 23, Reference 12, Theorems 1, Topology 2.

### Root files (3 modules)
